- [together/llm](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports/blob/main/reports/together/llm/2026-11-23.md) — The default base_url is `https://api.together.xyz/v1`, a host Together's docs no longer mention (since 2026-10-28, 3 weeks) — Together documents `https://api.together.ai/v1` for OpenAI-compatible clients; the `.xyz` host answered every probe this run and no deprecation is published for it — *needs a call: whether to move a working default host, given users may have the current one in allowlists*
- [together/llm](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports/blob/main/reports/together/llm/2026-11-23.md) — Together's recommended chat model is now moonshotai/Kimi-K3.1-Pro, which Pipecat has never evaluated (since 2026-11-09, 2 weeks) — still first on Together's recommended-models page for chat and reasoning, with GLM-5.3 an alternative; it worked through our class last run (TTFAT 1767 ms, median of 3, trivial prompt) but aiewf-eval still has no Kimi K3.1 row, only kimi-k2.6 — *needs a call: nothing to decide until aiewf-eval benchmarks Kimi K3.1 Pro; re-read the leaderboard next run*
- [together/stt](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports/blob/main/reports/together/stt/2026-11-23.md) — The endpoint's `prompt` biasing parameter is not exposed (since 2026-09-01, 11 weeks) — docs.together.ai/docs/speech-to-text still says prompts apply to Whisper-family models only and that other models accept but ignore them; earlier runs' raw-socket probes showed no transcript change on Whisper for either a one-word or a sentence-long prompt — *needs a call: evidence that the realtime endpoint actually applies the hint before adding a Settings field for it*
- [together/tts](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports/blob/main/reports/together/tts/2026-11-23.md) — Together's per-context messages (`context_id`, `context.cancel`) are unused, so an interruption clears the whole text buffer (since 2026-09-01, 11 weeks) — Pipecat audio context ids could be sent as Together `context_id` and cancelled with `context.cancel`; today every message rides the server's `default` context — *needs a call: whether mapping Pipecat's audio contexts onto Together contexts is worth it while the service only ever has one context open*
- [together/tts](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports/blob/main/reports/together/tts/2026-11-23.md) — Every settings change tears down the WebSocket, though Together updates voice, model and language in-session (since 2026-10-12, 6 weeks) — `_update_settings` disconnects and reconnects on any change; the reference marks `model`, `voice` and `language` as "Can also be set via `tts_session.updated`", so a mid-call voice switch costs a reconnect instead of one message — *needs a call: whether to special-case the in-session fields or keep one reconnect path for every setting*
- [together/tts](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports/blob/main/reports/together/tts/2026-11-23.md) — `extra_params` is documented as carrying only `pronunciation_dict`, but Together also documents `seed` and `instructions` — both keys already reach the provider through the existing dict (probed); only the `TogetherTTSSettings` docstring and the docs.pipecat.ai page name just `pronunciation_dict` — *needs a call: whether to enumerate provider keys in the docstring at all, given they are model-specific and Together adds them without notice*
- [ultravox/realtime](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports/blob/main/reports/ultravox/realtime/2026-11-23.md) — Several documented WebSocket messages are unimplemented and the call ID is never surfaced (since 2026-09-01, 11 weeks) — client-to-server `forced_agent_message`, `hang_up` and `spawn_thread` are never sent; `call_started` (carrying `callId` and `mediumRestriction`, seen in this run's debug log), `pong` and the thread/side-generation messages fall through to the debug log, so an app cannot correlate a session with Ultravox call history or recordings — *needs a call: which of these Pipecat should model at all — threads and forced messages have no frame-level equivalent today*
- [ultravox/realtime](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports/blob/main/reports/ultravox/realtime/2026-11-23.md) — `Settings.model` is inherited but never applied, and is ignored without warning at construction (since 2026-09-01, 11 weeks) — the model is fixed by `OneShotInputParams`; a model passed in `settings` at construction is silently dropped (runtime updates do warn). docs.pipecat.ai spells the inertness out — the Settings table marks the field unused and points at `OneShotInputParams.model` — so only the silent construction-time drop is left — *needs a call: whether to warn or reject an inert `Settings.model`*
- [ultravox/realtime](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports/blob/main/reports/ultravox/realtime/2026-11-23.md) — probe.py cannot construct `UltravoxRealtimeLLMService`, so the unit has no tier-2 probe (since 2026-09-01, 11 weeks) — the constructor takes a required `params` object rather than `api_key`/`model`, so `build_kwargs` raises TypeError ("missing 1 required keyword-only argument: 'params'") and every run needs an ad-hoc script to connect — *needs a call: a probe.py change (params-object support in `build_kwargs`) is watch tooling, not a service change*
- [whisper/stt](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports/blob/main/reports/whisper/stt/2026-11-23.md) — `Model` enum lags faster-whisper's alias table (since 2026-09-01, 11 weeks) — faster-whisper 1.2.1 resolves 19 aliases; `Model` carries 8 and is missing `distil-large-v3.5`, `distil-large-v3`, `distil-small.en`, the `.en` sizes and the `large-v3-turbo`/`turbo` shorthands. `Settings.model` is a plain string, so this is discoverability, not a gate. — *needs a call: which of faster-whisper's aliases belong in a curated enum*
- [whisper/stt](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports/blob/main/reports/whisper/stt/2026-11-23.md) — `Model.LARGE_V3_TURBO` points at a third-party CT2 conversion (since 2026-09-01, 11 weeks) — the enum holds `deepdml/faster-whisper-large-v3-turbo-ct2`, while faster-whisper resolves its own `large-v3-turbo` alias to `mobiuslabsgmbh/faster-whisper-large-v3-turbo` — the repo the service's own unsupported-language error tells users to switch to. — *needs a call: changing the value swaps which weights users download, so it needs a call on doing that outside a major release*
- [whisper/stt](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports/blob/main/reports/whisper/stt/2026-11-23.md) — `WhisperMLXSTTSettings.engine` is a documented setting that nothing reads — `engine` defaults to `"mlx"` and appears in the Settings docstring and on docs.pipecat.ai, but `WhisperSTTServiceMLX.run_stt` never reads it — `mlx_whisper.transcribe` gets only the audio, `path_or_hf_repo`, `temperature` and `language`. mlx-whisper 0.4.3 has no engine parameter, so there is nothing to pass it to. — *needs a call: whether to deprecate and remove the field or keep it as an inert compatibility knob; it is public API, so removal needs a deprecation cycle*
- [xai/llm](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports/blob/main/reports/xai/llm/2026-11-23.md) — Default grok-4.20-non-reasoning is older than the grok-4.7 flagship (since 2026-09-01, 11 weeks) — grok-4.7 is still xAI's recommended chat model and the grok-4.20 line still carries no deprecation marker; reasoning still cannot be disabled on grok-4.7 (documented effort values low/medium/high (default)/xhigh, "Reasoning cannot be disabled"), and this run's single samples put it at 2052 ms TTFAT with 1056 ms of thinking against 620 ms for the default on a trivial prompt — *needs a call: aiewf-eval's medium-context leaderboard still carries no Grok row, so neither model has a benchmarked pass rate; re-check the leaderboard each run*
- [xai/realtime](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports/blob/main/reports/xai/realtime/2026-11-23.md) — `audio/opus` is not among the accepted codecs (since 2026-09-01, 11 weeks) — xAI accepts `audio/opus` (24 kHz mono, one raw Opus packet per payload) for input and output; Pipecat models only PCM, PCMU and PCMA — *needs a call: Opus packets would have to be decoded before they can become `TTSAudioRawFrame`, so this is more than a new format class*
- [xai/realtime](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports/blob/main/reports/xai/realtime/2026-11-23.md) — Sessions run at xAI's reasoning default of `high` rather than the lowest documented setting (since 2026-11-09, 2 weeks) — CONTRIBUTING's reasoning-defaults convention sends each listed model the lowest setting its provider documents; xAI documents `reasoning.effort` as `high` (default) or `none`, a live `session.created` confirms `{"effort":"high"}` on a session the service never configures, and the previous run's A/B put time-to-first-audio at 855 ms median with `high` against 741 ms with `none` (3 runs each, overlapping spreads), so the latency case is weak — *needs a call: a call on whether speech-to-speech services should adopt the reasoning-defaults convention at all — no realtime service in the repo keeps a defaults table today*
- [xai/realtime](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports/blob/main/reports/xai/realtime/2026-11-23.md) — `force_message` sends its content part as `text` where the spec documents `output_text` — the `force_message` branch of `conversation.item.create` restricts the content-part type to the single value `output_text`, and llm.py:525 builds `ItemContent(type="text", ...)`; a live probe showed xAI accepts both today (audio deltas, no error either way), so nothing is broken until xAI tightens validation to its own enum — *needs a call: a one-word change, but it is a wire-format change the probe shows nobody needs today, so it is worth confirming xAI treats the looser value as supported rather than tolerated*
- [xai/realtime](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports/blob/main/reports/xai/realtime/2026-11-23.md) — The `voice` docstring claims "eve" is xAI's own default, which it is not — `SessionProperties.voice` defaults to "eve" and its docstring explains that as "xAI's own default", but a `session.created` snapshot taken before any `session.update` reports `Ara`, and xAI documents no default voice for Realtime (Eve is the default for the TTS endpoints only); the Pipecat-side default is unaffected, only the explanation is wrong — *needs a call: a call on whether to correct the parenthetical or to follow xAI and stop pinning a voice, which would change the voice existing bots speak with*
- [xai/stt](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports/blob/main/reports/xai/stt/2026-11-23.md) — The verified language map covers 16 codes; xAI documents 38 base codes plus 7 regional variants (since 2026-09-01, 11 weeks) — missing bg, bs, ca, cs, da, el, fa, fi, fil, hr, hu, mk, ms, nb, nl, pl, ro, sk, sv, th, uk, ur, yue, and the regional es-ES, es-MX, pt-BR, pt-PT, ar-AE, ar-EG, ar-SA; all reach the API through `resolve_language`'s base-code fallback but log a "not verified" warning every session. The published list is unchanged since last run. — *needs a call: whether the regional codes should map through as-is — `Language.ES_ES` sends `es` today and would start sending `es-ES`, a behavior change — and the same probe blocker as `format`*
- [xai/tts](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports/blob/main/reports/xai/tts/2026-11-23.md) — XAIHttpTTSService sends the raw Language value instead of the xAI language map (since 2026-09-01, 11 weeks) — `run_tts` builds `payload["language"]` with `str(self._settings.language)`, so `Language.EN_US` goes out as `en-US`; the WebSocket class runs the same value through `language_to_xai_language`, which falls back to the base code `en` and warns. The API accepted `en-US` in an earlier probe, so nothing breaks today — the two classes just behave differently. — *needs a call: is the verbatim pass-through deliberate (xAI accepts codes beyond its listed set, with varying accuracy), or should the HTTP class use the same map as the WebSocket one?*
- [xtts/tts](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports/blob/main/reports/xtts/tts/2026-11-23.md) — Arabic is missing from the XTTS language map (since 2026-09-01, 11 weeks) — XTTS-v2 serves ar; `LANGUAGE_MAP` in `xtts/tts.py` has the other 16 codes, so `Language.AR` only reaches the server through the base-code fallback and logs a "not verified" warning — *needs a call: whether a deprecated service due for removal in 2.0.0 is worth touching, and no XTTS server is reachable to probe the fix*
- [zoom_ai/realtime](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports/blob/main/reports/zoom_ai/realtime/2026-11-23.md) — `think.reasoning` is sent on every session.update, though Zoom documents reasoning as a first-update setting (since 2026-11-09, 2 weeks) — `_build_session` gates `provider` and `listen` on the first update but not `reasoning`; the live-mode field table still reads "Set the reasoning level, such as low, in your first session.update" — *needs a call: unknown whether the server rejects or silently ignores reasoning in a later update; confirming needs a session with a key*
- [zoom_ai/stt](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports/blob/main/reports/zoom_ai/stt/2026-11-23.md) — Captions (`config.interim_results` / `transcription.delta`) are not consumed, so there are no interim transcripts (since 2026-11-09, 2 weeks) — Zoom still marks partial results as preview; each delta carries the accumulated text for its `item_id`, so it would map to `InterimTranscriptionFrame` with replace-not-append semantics. Captions and Text Polishing are independent: with both on, deltas are unpolished and completed events carry the polished text. — *needs a call: wait for the feature to leave preview, then a decision on emitting interim frames*
- [zoom_ai/stt](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports/blob/main/reports/zoom_ai/stt/2026-11-23.md) — `config.profanity_filter` has no Settings field (since 2026-11-09, 2 weeks) — documented Live boolean, echoed back as `profanity_filter_enabled` in `session.updated`; today it is reachable only as `extra={"config": {"profanity_filter": True}}` — a one-line pass-through field would do it — *needs a call: a probe with the field set against the live API before proposing the field; no ZOOM_AI_API_KEY in this environment*
- [zoom_ai/tts](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports/blob/main/reports/zoom_ai/tts/2026-11-23.md) — A 429 or 503 ends the sentence instead of honoring `Retry-After` — Zoom documents `Retry-After` on `RATE_LIMIT_EXCEEDED` (429) and `SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE` (503, also `metadata.retry_after_seconds`) and asks clients to back off and retry; `_synthesize` pushes an error and returns, so the bot silently skips that sentence — *needs a call: a call on whether a TTS service should retry at all here — Pipecat TTS services generally surface the error and let the app react — and no ZOOM_AI_API_KEY to probe the throttled path*

</details>

---
To record a decision about a change to consider, reply on this issue naming the service and enough of the item to identify it, one decision per line — e.g. `deepgram/stt, diarize_model: skip, the extra= workaround is fine` or `openai/realtime, tool_choice: tracked in #5400, stop reporting`. Fixes need no comment: the next run sees the code change. It reads these comments for everything it cannot see — won't do, later, tracked elsewhere — and records each decision in the service's `decisions.md`, beside its reports. Full triage workflow: the [README](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports#the-weekly-loop).
